lines:
  - id: f1095a.h_no_b_issue
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Column B is not blank or zero in a month with a column A amount, there was no unreported change in circumstances, and there is no other reason to believe the Marketplace reported the wrong applicable SLCSP premium"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1095a.b_blank, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f1095a.b_changed, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f1095a.b_doubt, {const: false}]}
  - id: f1095a.uniform
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Full-year coverage with no changes on Form 1095-A, Part III, column A or B: the enrollment premium was the same for every month of 2025 and the (correct applicable) SLCSP premium is the same for every month of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1095a.h_no_b_issue, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f1095a.same_months, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1095a.h_no_b_issue, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f1095a.b_fixed_same, {const: true}]}
    note: "Open: the line 10 test also requires enrollment for all 12 months. No separate input asks this; the reading taken is that same_months (the same column A amount in every month, January to December) covers it. A form with column B blank or zero, an unreported change, or another doubt about column B is uniform only if the corrected column B, and column A, are the same in every month (b_fixed_same). A void or superseded form is not handled here; Form 8962 screens it through f1095a.void."

    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [f1095a.uniform, {const: true}]}, then: f1095a.c_total}
        - {guard: {op: test, eq: [f1095a.uniform, {const: false}]}, then: f1095a.h_c_sum}
    note: "Line 33 is the total of lines 21 through 32. When the form is uniform, the supplied line 33 column C amount is used. Otherwise the monthly column C amounts are added."

Second transcription's note: Open: the line 10 test also requires enrollment for all 12 months. No separate input asks this; the reading taken is that same_months (the same column A amount in every month, January to December) covers it. A form with column B blank or zero, an unreported change, or another doubt about column B is uniform only if the corrected column B, and column A, are the same in every month (b_fixed_same). A void or superseded form is not handled here; Form 8962 screens it through f1095a.void.

Ruling: open
Reviewed in: pass ea49629f14d1
